## 📷 例 1：沙漠绿洲旅人（Cinematic Desert Oasis Traveler）

![沙漠绿洲旅人](../images/Cinematic%20Desert%20Oasis%20Traveler.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
Create a cinematic photorealistic wide shot of {argument name="subject" default="a weary desert traveler"} kneeling at the edge of a shallow oasis pool, leaning forward and cupping water in both hands to drink or wash their face. The subject wears layered, dusty, earth-toned nomadic robes, a wrapped turban, loose trousers, worn fabric scarves, and rough travel clothing, with wet hands and streams of water dripping back into the pool, creating small splashes and concentric ripples. The face is intentionally obscured by a plain dark brown square censor block centered over the head, while the body language remains intimate and exhausted. Set the scene in a lush desert oasis with {argument name="background setting" default="tall palm trees, reeds, shrubs, smooth stones, and reflective still water"}; the low {argument name="time of day" default="golden sunrise"} shines from the right side through the palms, creating warm backlight, lens glow, long shadows, misty atmosphere, and shimmering reflections on the water. Use a dramatic documentary film still style, ultra-realistic textures, shallow depth of field, warm amber and sepia color grading, high dynamic range, detailed fabric fibers, visible droplets, natural dust and grime, 16:9 composition, subject placed on the left foreground with the oasis stretching into the bright background, shot as if on a cinema camera with anamorphic softness and realistic environmental lighting.
```

**来源：** [@jznode](https://x.com/jznode/status/2052452475844559301) | 2026-05-08

## 📷 例 2：马尔代夫水上别墅奢华视觉效果

**Prompt:**

```text
一张旨在激发即时预订欲望的震撼豪华酒店营销照片，一张马尔代夫五星级度假村黄金时段的超高端 {argument name="resort type" default="水上别墅套房"} 的绝美室内广角镜头。套房的整个前墙由落地式可伸缩玻璃面板组成，完全向外敞开，将室内空间与自然环境无缝融合。套房内，一张铺着清爽白色埃及棉床品并整齐摆放着装饰靠垫的特大号床正对着开阔的海景。一张白色亚麻日间床，以及放在低矮柚木边桌上、盛有两杯香槟和冰镇香槟桶的组合，被放置在抛光白色大理石室内地面与室外天然柚木露台的交界处。露台直接延伸至下方清澈见底的绿松石色泻湖水面上，透过晶莹剔透的浅水可以清晰地看到沙质湖底。露台边缘设有一个私人无边泳池，池水颜色与泻湖完美融合，与远方的海洋地平线融为一体。地平线呈现出完美的渐变色彩，从水天一线的浓郁琥珀色，过渡到炽热的珊瑚橙，再到顶部的深紫色，第一颗晚星刚刚显现。温暖的黄金时段光线洒满套房内部的每一个白色表面，散发出浓郁的琥珀色光芒，专业豪华酒店摄影品质，16:9 横向比例。
```

## 📷 例 33：woman with a cat in her arms

![woman with a cat in her arms](../images/woman%20with%20a%20cat%20in%20her%20arms.png)

**Prompt:**

```text
A hyper-realistic photograph of a woman taking a mirror selfie in her bedroom using a smartphone. She has her original hairstyle with loose, slightly messy strands of hair softly falling over her face in a cool, effortlessly chic way with good volume. She is wearing a vintage-style spaghetti-strap crop top and high-waisted denim shorts. She is cradling a fluffy orange tabby cat in her arms. Shot with the aesthetic of an early-2000s digital camera, using only natural light, no flash. Soft late-afternoon golden sunlight streaming through the window, gently hitting her skin, face, and the cat’s fur, creating warm natural highlights and glows. Slightly warm yellow-orange-gray tone, subtle digital noise and grain for that authentic vintage digital look. Half-body mirror shot, focus sharply on her face and the cat. Background shows a simple white bed and plain walls with soft patches of sunlight and gentle shadows. Overall atmosphere is warm, natural, unposed, and intimate, like a casual candid snapshot from the 2000s.
```

**来源：** [@Reddit](https://www.reddit.com/r/ChatGPT/comments/1t0qio9/gpt_image_2_has_created_this_is_there_any_problem/) | 2026-05-13

Johnson” – 下边缘设有机器可读的身份识别条。其他设计风格：– 柔和的蓝色、金色和中性柔和渐变色 – 带有逼真反射的聚碳酸酯层压卡片质感 – 简洁的政府视觉语言。灯光柔和且均匀漫射，强调全息效果、雕刻纹理和逼真的印刷细节，无刺眼阴影。画面中无手部出现。风格：超细节、照片级真实感、8K 分辨率、微距清晰度、专业产品摄影、纪录片式写实、高度逼真的政府身份证设计。

## 📷 例 43：Bangkok Night Street Photography

![Bangkok Night Street Photography](../images/Bangkok%20Night%20Street%20Photography.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
A street photograph capturing a {argument name="subject" default="young Thai woman"} cutting through the dense nighttime crowd on {argument name="location" default="Khao San Road, Bangkok"}. The subject is caught mid-stride wearing a {argument name="clothing" default="loose floral dress"}, expression distracted and completely natural, not performing for the camera, small crossbody bag across her shoulder. The environment is dense and chaotic: bar signs in Thai and English stacked vertically, tuk-tuks and motorbikes pressing through foot traffic, tourists and locals blending in the moving crowd around her. Lighting is messy and authentic: uneven bar fluorescent spill, LED signage scatter, occasional reflected flash from the crowd, no clean single source. Composition: full-body medium shot from slightly above eye level, subject in sharp focus in left center, surrounding crowd in natural motion blur, neon signs stretching tall in the vertical frame above. Color palette: acidic yellow, electric pink, deep orange — intense but documentary-accurate neon saturation, not stylized. Render with photographic realism: subtle film grain, avoid over-smoothed CG textures. Skin: subsurface scattering and micro-texture. Preserve authentic motion blur on background crowd, not artificial bokeh. Photorealistic rendering, no watermark.
```

![皮克斯风格咖啡品牌项目](../images/皮克斯风格咖啡品牌项目.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[Core Requirements] A portrait of a person in a {argument name="clothing color" default="light pastel pink"} sleeveless top under the warm golden sunlight of late afternoon. Maintain realistic proportions.

[Person's Pose and Expression] [Person Reference Images 1-11] The subject is standing in {argument name="pose" default="profile"} with a dreamy and slightly sad gaze toward the camera. The person looks at the camera only with their eyes, with their head slightly lowered and turned. Hair naturally flutters around the face due to the wind blowing from behind.

[Hair and Makeup Details] A dark-haired bun style tied loosely at the back, but many strands of hair are naturally scattered over the face and neck. The makeup is transparent and clean, with a natural blush on the cheeks and lips in a moist, natural color. Fine beads of sweat are visible on the skin.

[Lighting and Direction] A warm and intense halo light enters through the hair from the center of the frame, wrapping the woman's hair and facial contours in a golden glow. Light reflects off the skin, giving it a sheen.

[Texture and Color Mood] A soft, dreamy atmosphere and graininess, as if shot with a film camera. Colors harmonize warm gold, green, and soft light blue. The fine texture of the skin and the cotton texture of the sleeveless clothing are felt.

[Film and Camera Lens Depth/Angle] Shallow depth of field makes the subject sharp while the background is softly blurred. Captured from the front in a medium close-up. A soft and warm depiction characteristic of old-fashioned film lenses.

[Background Elements (Optional)] Blurred green leaves are visible in the background.

[Signature] A small Threads logo (operated by Meta) is located in the bottom right, with "{argument name="signature" default="CHAse"}" written small like a signature in white cursive above it.

### 📷 例 112：带有日式手写标注的早餐俯拍图

![带有日式手写标注的早餐俯拍图](../images/带有日式手写标注的早餐俯拍图.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
目标：创作一张温馨的早餐俯拍照片，配有手写日记风格的日文标注，营造出适合温暖清晨社交媒体帖子的氛围。画面应具有真实感、柔和的后期处理效果以及淡淡的怀旧气息。

画布：16:9 宽屏横向图片，尺寸约为 1024×576。背景使用棕白格纹桌布，配以柔和的晨光、轻柔的阴影以及略带温暖的胶片质感。

主体布局：放置 2 个主要的食物/饮品区。左侧是一个装饰性的红白瓷茶杯，盛着黑咖啡，放在配套的托盘上。右侧是一个白色大方盘，盛放早餐食物。左上角留出深色的负空间，用于书写标题。

食物与物品数量：盘子区域内包含 7 个可见的早餐物品/道具：1 叠烤面包片、1 根剥了一半皮的熟香蕉、1 小堆格兰诺拉麦片、1 个透明玻璃碗盛着浅黄色苹果酱（顶部有 1 颗深色西梅）、1 个棕色鸡蛋、1 个位于鸡蛋后方的小木制蛋杯或木制杯垫，以及 1 个白色方盘。吐司应作为盘子的视觉中心，呈现金黄的烤制表面和厚实的白面包边。香蕉应位于盘子左上方。鸡蛋应位于盘子右上方。玻璃碗应位于右下方。格兰诺拉麦片应位于吐司和玻璃碗之间。

手写文字与标注数量：添加 9 组白色/黑色墨水的手写标注，配有箭头、圆圈、小爱心、闪光符号和涂鸦。保留日文文本作为装饰性手写内容：
1. 左上角大标题：「わたしの整う朝ごはん。」
2. 标题下方副标题：「シンプルだけど、ちゃんと満たされる。」
3. 咖啡附近的圆形注释：「今日も はじまりは 深い一杯から。」
4. 咖啡下方的小注释：「ほっと息をつく、大切な時間。」
5. 左下角带有粉色胶带的撕纸便签：「朝の光を浴びて、心もからだも ゆっくり目覚めていく。」
6. 香蕉上方带箭头的注释：「完熟バナナで やさしい甘みを チャージ。」
7. 鸡蛋旁边的气泡注释：「たんぱく質で 1日を すっきり スタート！」
8. 吐司附近带箭头的注释：「サクッと焼けた トーストが 一番のごちそう。」
9. 右下方围绕格兰诺拉麦片、苹果酱和整体感受的注释：「ザクザクのグラノーラで 食感と栄養をプラス。」、「りんごとプルーンで 自然な甘さを からだにやさしく。」以及「ちゃんと食べると、心までふんわり軽くなる。」

视觉风格：写实的早餐摄影与随性的手绘叠加图形相结合。大部分手写文字使用白色墨水，盘子上的食物标签使用黑色墨水，并配以虚线气泡、弯曲箭头、小星星、小爱心和俏皮的下划线。字体应看起来像手写，而非数字化字体。标注应清晰可见，但带有自然的不完美感。

可自定义元素：标题应显示为 {argument name="headline text" default="わたしの整う朝ごはん。"}；饮品应为 {argument name="morning drink" default="红瓷杯中的黑咖啡"}；主食应为 {argument name="main breakfast food" default="厚实金黄的吐司"}；水果应为 {argument name="fruit item" default="熟香蕉和配有西梅的苹果酱"}；整体氛围应为 {argument name="mood" default="温柔、温馨、正念的清晨"}。

约束条件：保持构图整洁且令人食欲大开，不要添加超出规定数量的额外食物，不要出现人物或手部，避免出现 Logo 或水印，并将日文手写字自然地融入图片中，就像一张经过精心编辑的早餐日记照片。
```

### 📷 例 119：鱼市追猫 CCD 街拍

![鱼市追猫 CCD 街拍](../images/鱼市追猫%20CCD%20街拍.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
Use the uploaded reference image as the exact identity base for the main subject. Preserve their authentic facial structure, recognizable appearance, hairstyle, body proportions, skin texture, and overall identity with high consistency. Completely ignore any unrelated background from the original image.

Create a hyper-chaotic early-2000s Japanese digicam snapshot aesthetic with raw paparazzi energy and accidental comedy.

Scene: the subject is sprinting wildly through a busy city street while yelling and laughing in pure chaos, desperately trying to catch a mischievous cat that just stole a large shiny fish from a market stall. The cat is gripping the slippery silver fish tightly in its mouth while running directly toward the camera.

Composition must prioritize TWO focal subjects:

1. PRIMARY FOCUS → the cat's face
2. SECONDARY FOCUS → the human subject's face

The cat dominates the foreground frame with its face pushed absurdly close into the lens, partially cropped near the lower-right side. Massive fisheye distortion stretches its features dramatically - huge bulging eyes, exaggerated whiskers, detailed fur texture, fish flapping violently with droplets and motion streaks flying outward.

Behind the cat, the subject is charging forward aggressively with intense energy, reaching toward the animal mid-run. Their expression is loud, chaotic, and comedic, eyes locked directly onto the cat to create strong interaction and pursuit tension.

Styling:
Randomized layered Y2K Tokyo streetwear inspired by chaotic Shibuya nightlife fashion - oversized zip hoodies, vintage mesh layers, mini bags, low-rise pieces, striped arm warmers, chunky accessories, early-2000s sneaker styling, messy fashionable youth aesthetic.

Camera & framing:
extreme low-angle fisheye lens, ultra-wide invasive perspective, severe Dutch angle, asymmetrical framing, warped spatial distortion, exaggerated depth compression, aggressive foreshortening.

Motion treatment:
insanely intense movement blur with radial streaking, shutter drag, CCD sensor smearing, directional speed trails, accidental camera shake, stretched highlights, warped moving objects, imperfect chaotic snapshot timing.

Environment:
sunny crowded urban street with pigeons exploding into flight across the frame, scattered newspapers and debris flying through the air, warped pedestrians, market elements smeared by motion, overexposed sunlight leaking into one corner of the frame.

Lighting & texture:
harsh direct flash combined with bright outdoor sunlight, dirty CCD digicam texture, blown highlights, chromatic aberration, digital noise, bloom artifacts, compressed early-2000s camera look, gritty nostalgic cyber-chaos atmosphere.

Ultra-raw candid energy, messy composition, humorous accidental masterpiece aesthetic.
```

**来源：** [@mehvishs25](https://x.com/mehvishs25/status/2058375167550845263) | 2026-05-26

### 📷 例 138：电影感鱼眼城市人像

![电影感鱼眼城市人像](../images/电影感鱼眼城市人像.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
仅使用我上传的人像作为身份参考。严格保留我真实的五官特征、比例、脸型、发型、发际线、肤色以及整体气质。不要使用参考图中的面部、性别特征或外貌。仅将参考图用于镜头语言、姿势、场景、构图、光影和氛围。创作一张超写实的城市街拍照片：人物坐在现代城市建筑入口的地面或台阶上，采用超广角鱼眼镜头进行低角度拍摄。相机极度贴近地面，营造出强烈的空间弯曲畸变。人物位于画面下方中心附近，一只手自然地伸向镜头，手掌非常靠近镜头，以创造出戏剧性的前景透视感。另一条腿自然弯曲。姿势要显得放松自然，并带有真实、柔和的微笑。场景为现代城市街道，周围环绕着高楼、玻璃门、台阶、树木和远处的建筑，所有景物都被鱼眼效果轻微拉伸。黄金时刻的阳光从主体后方射入，形成自然的逆光、镜头光晕和长长的阴影。整体光影温暖且具有电影感。图像应看起来像真实的运动相机或超广角智能手机照片，具有逼真的皮肤纹理、自然的毛孔、可信的阴影以及融合良好的环境光。不要过度平滑皮肤。拒绝塑料感，拒绝动漫感。服装应为休闲夏季街头风格，自动搭配适合我外貌的服饰，例如 {argument name="clothing item" default="简约白 T 恤"}、{argument name="bottoms" default="短裤"} 和运动鞋。保持自然，不要过度造型。最终图像应具有真实、抓拍、充满活力和纪实感，采用 4:5 竖构图，无水印、无文字、无 Logo。负面提示词：水印、文字、Logo、AI 标签、动漫风格、卡通风格、塑料皮肤、过度平滑的皮肤、假脸、换脸感、相似度低、五官扭曲、手部畸形、多指、手掌变形、肢体扭曲、低分辨率、模糊、过度曝光、过度锐化、不真实的光影、贴图感、影棚人像感、浓妆、透视错误、背景杂乱
```

```text
主題：春の神社で白狐をまとう日系狐仙ポートレート / kitsune portrait

人物・表情：黒髪の若い女性、日系らしい自然な顔立ち、繊細な前髪と上品なお団子ヘア、白い狐耳、透明感のある白い肌、細長く艶っぽい目元、やわらかく惹きつける視線、ほんのり微笑む上品な表情、瞳の内側に虹色の屈折光が宿り、その光が下まぶたから目尻へ自然ににじむ神秘的な目元、ナチュラルで洗練された和風メイク

服装・ポーズ：白と金を基調にした華やかな和装、繊細な金刺繍が入った着物、ややゆるく開いた上品な襟元で色気を控えめに演出、胸元は自然にふくよか、指先を唇元に添えたしなやかなポーズ、肩元に寄り添う白狐の精霊、足元付近に狐面

構図・カメラ：上半身中心の縦構図、正面寄りの構図、人物の顔と目を主役にした視線誘導、白狐を左側に寄り添わせたバランスのよい配置、85mmのポートレートレンズ、浅い被写界深度、顔は高精細でシャープ、背景は自然なぼけ、SNSで映える印象的な一枚

質感・スタイル：リアルな写真表現と幻想表現を融合した日系ファンタジーポートレート、高精細、4K品質、毛穴や肌理、唇の質感、鼻筋や目元の陰影まで自然に見える写真的な肌、CG感を抑えた上質な仕上がり、白狐の毛並みは一本一本やわらかく繊細、目元の虹色光は装飾模様ではなく瞳から屈折して流れ出すように表現、胸元や背景の光演出は控えめにして顔・瞳・白狐へ視線を集中、かわいさと神秘感、上品な艶っぽさを両立した雰囲気

ネガティブ：低画質、不自然な顔、不自然な目、不自然な手、余分な指、欠けた指、歪んだ指、崩れた顔、過度に大きい目、強すぎるCG感、アニメ塗り、不自然な肌補正、貼り付いたようなアイメイク、過剰な発光、情報量過多な背景、破綻した着物、不自然な胸元、重複した動物、文字、ロゴ、透かし

### 📷 例 148：蓝天下的夏日跳跃人像

![蓝天下的夏日跳跃人像](../images/蓝天下的夏日跳跃人像.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
主題：青空を跳ねる金髪女性のサマーポートレート / summer jump portrait

人物・表情：金髪の若い女性、セイバー風を思わせる上品なお団子アレンジ、やわらかな前髪、自然な日系メイク、明るく爽やかな笑顔、透明感のある表情、自然なカメラ目線

Center the composition on exactly two punk-styled young adults standing confidently side by side, seen full body from below: on the left, {argument name="male character style" default="a tall slim man with pale blond spiky pigtail hair, visible neck and leg tattoos, layered necklaces, a white T-shirt with a red target logo, oversized white shorts, red-striped white socks, yellow high-top sneakers, and a loose blue coat covered in large cream polka dots and red target patches"}; on the right, {argument name="female character style" default="a tall slim woman with windblown black hair, red sunglasses, hoop earrings, tattoos, a cropped white top and white skirt or shorts, a vivid red oversized jacket with target patches, matching red-striped white socks, and yellow high-top sneakers"}. Include exactly two dogs: one large scruffy white terrier on the far left wearing black sunglasses, standing alert with tail raised, and one small tan dog on the far right sitting calmly on a red leash. 她身着一件 {argument name="qipao color" default="淡鼠尾草绿"} 的无袖改良旗袍——一种柔和、略带灰调的竹青色，饱和度不高——立领配有浅灰绿色的盘扣，裙摆剪裁宽松，自然垂落至小腿中部。双手自然垂在身体两侧，右手靠近栏杆。木桥板因年代久远和潮湿而显得深暗；荷花池填满了下方背景，圆形的荷叶在盛夏中层叠繁茂。远处的岸边是灰色的园林石墙和隐约的垂柳。她鬓角的两三根碎发被水园的湿气轻轻吹乱，呈现出不规则的柔和弧度。
晨光从左侧投射而来——清晨冷暖交替的光线，在桥板上投下长而柔和的阴影，并捕捉到下方荷叶的边缘。水面反射提供了二次散射补光——光线苍白而漫射——柔化了桥栏下方的阴影区域，赋予画面一种特别柔和的亮度。整体氛围宁静从容，展现了园林私密的清晨时刻。阴影区域和深色木桥纹理中带有细微的 ISO 400 胶片颗粒感，呈现出摄影噪点质感，而非 CG 渲染的平滑感。长宽比 2:3。无水印，无文字覆盖，非卡通，非数字绘画，非插画，非动漫。

Authentic daily life photography, natural candid moment. A three-quarter length portrait of a {argument name="subject" default="young woman"} mid-stride on a narrow wooden zigzag bridge (九曲桥) crossing a classical {argument name="location" default="Suzhou garden lotus pond"}. She is in a gentle walking pause — one foot slightly ahead, gaze turned downward toward the water. Large dark-green lotus leaves spread below, rain-beaded at their edges; the water between shows a pale sky reflection. An age-darkened low wooden balustrade runs at her right.
East Asian young woman in her early 20s. Almond-shaped eyes cast downward with a small thoughtful expression — neither smiling nor unsmiling, simply present and absorbed. Straight refined nose. Fair skin in the cool morning garden light. Elegant upswept chignon with two thin crossed hairpins at the crown, a small white flower tucked near the ear. Light elegant makeup — near-bare coverage, lips in a barely-tinted neutral. Skin subsurface scattering visible even in the cooler morning light, a faint internal warmth at the cheeks, specular micro-highlights subdued and soft, fine foundation powder grain perceptible.
She wears a sleeveless modern-improved qipao in {argument name="qipao color" default="pale sage-green"} — a muted, slightly dusty bamboo tone, not saturated — mandarin collar with pale grey-green frog closures, the skirt cut with a slightly more generous soft drape falling to mid-calf. Both hands are relaxed at her sides, the right near the balustrade. The wooden bridge planks are dark with age and moisture; the lotus pond fills the lower background, the round leaves layered in lush summer abundance. The far bank shows grey garden wall stone and the suggestion of weeping willow. Two or three stray hairs at her temple have been gently displaced by the water-garden air, their small curves irregular and soft.
Morning light is directional from the left — a cool-warm transition of early day, casting long gentle shadows across the bridge planks and catching the outer edges of lotus leaves below. Water surface reflections add a secondary scattered fill — pale and diffused — softening the shadow areas beneath the bridge rail and giving the scene a particularly gentle luminosity. The overall mood is peaceful and unhurried, the garden in a private morning moment. Subtle ISO 400 film grain in the shadow areas of the lotus pond and darker bridge wood tones, photographic noise texture not CG render smoothness. Aspect ratio 2:3. No watermark, no text overlay, not cartoon, not digitally painted, not illustration, not anime.

人物穿着 藕粉珍珠金系的轻纱仙侠层叠裙，服装属于 华丽唐风幻想古装体系，华丽度为 中高。服装主色为 藕粉 / 樱粉，辅色为 奶白 / 浅胭脂粉，点缀色为 浅金 / 珍珠白。服装结构为轻盈柔美的女主式层叠纱裙，可采用柔和弧形领或幻想式对襟结构，外层覆盖半透明轻纱与刺绣薄纱，内层为柔软绢感或雪纺质地，层次丰富但不厚重。袖型为 轻纱广袖或垂坠纱袖，袖摆轻盈流动。衣身和袖口带有精致 花卉纹、藤蔓纹、细腻宝相纹 刺绣，辅以珍珠点缀和浅金刺绣细节。腰部有精致腰封、垂坠珠链、浅金腰饰与珍珠装饰，整体服装轻盈、柔软、通透、仙气十足，华丽而不俗，梦幻而不杂乱。

## 📷 例 422：复古 CCD 数码相机抓拍

![复古 CCD 数码相机抓拍](../images/%E5%A4%8D%E5%8F%A4%20CCD%20%E6%95%B0%E7%A0%81%E7%9B%B8%E6%9C%BA%E6%8A%93%E6%8B%8D.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
一张充满生活气息的街头抓拍。画面主体是一位{argument name="年龄" default="20岁出头"}的{argument name="人种" default="东亚女孩"}，气质清新甜美，像邻家妹妹。她有着深棕色的及肩发，随意地在脑后扎成一个蓬松的低丸子头或低马尾，额前是稀疏的空气刘海，整体给人一种不经意的亲切感和初恋感。 女孩的脸部特写：她正侧身回眸，对着镜头露出一个极其灿烂、毫无防备的笑容，眼睛笑成了弯弯的月牙，眼神清澈明亮。她的妆容非常淡雅自然，重点是水光感的粉色唇釉，让嘴唇看起来饱满晶莹。皮肤质感白皙通透，在光线下呈现出一种“玻璃皮肤”的光泽感，尤其在左侧脸颊和鼻梁处有明显的高光。她穿着一件浅色碎花图案的无袖挂脖上衣，露出了圆润的肩头和纤细的锁骨，姿态自然放松。 环境背景设定在傍晚时分的城市街头，像是在{argument name="地点" default="北京的胡同口"}或某个有生活气息的街区。女孩身后是正在过马路的场景，左侧远处有一辆蓝色的公交车和骑电动车的人，稍近处一个戴着口罩的男生正骑着一辆蓝白相间的共享单车，这些动态元素给画面增添了故事感。右侧有一辆深蓝色汽车的车尾，更远处是绿色的行道树和一些中式风格的屋檐。整个背景有一定的景深，处于轻微的动态模糊和失焦状态，突出了前景的人物。 情绪氛围是夏日傍晚的慵懒与惬意，混合了千禧年复古的怀旧感和日系写真的清新感。这是一张充满偶然性的旅行快照或生活记录，捕捉了不期而遇的美好瞬间，充满了松弛和真实的呼吸感。 摄影和画质重点：这张照片要严格模仿一台2000年代早期的消费级CCD数码相机（例如佳能IXUS或索尼Cyber-shot）的直出效果。分辨率相对较低，画质有轻微的压缩感和模糊感，绝非现代高清精修大片。细节，尤其是背景和头发边缘，锐度不足，保留老旧传感器特有的粗糙噪点和颗粒感（Grain）。光线来源是傍晚时分的漫射天光（Dappled light），整体光线均匀柔和。光线从女孩的左前方打来，在她的左侧脸颊、鼻梁和额头形成了明显的高光区域，局部轻微过曝（Overexposure），正是这种过曝营造了水嫩的皮肤质感和CCD特有的光晕感。她的眼睛里有明亮的眼神光（Catchlight）。整体色彩饱和度偏低，但色调偏暖，带有一点粉色和黄色的倾向，天空部分因过曝而泛白，形成了低对比度、柔和阴影（Soft shadows）的复古胶片滤镜感。强调这是一张非精心构图的抓拍，保留了画面的不完美和真实性。 构图采用竖构图的半身肖像。机位与人物视线基本持平，略带一点仰拍视角。人物位于画面中心偏右的位置，回眸的姿态形成一条优美的动态引导线。背景中的街道、车辆和行人作为环境元素，丰富了画面层次，营造出强烈的空间纵深感和现场感。 比例「3：4」
```

This aura acts as a powerful rim and backlight, highlighting the smoke, dust, dnd silhouette. The setting is minimal and stark: a concrete or desolate paved surface meeting a heavily overcast sky. ### 📷 例 436：角色记忆相册

![角色记忆相册](../images/%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E8%AE%B0%E5%BF%86%E7%9B%B8%E5%86%8C.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
提供された画像をオリジナルキャラクターの基準リファレンスとして使用すること。参照画像のキャラクターの「中学生時代の思い出アルバム」を作成する。これは設定資料集ではない。これは公式プロフィールではない。これは本人が中学生時代に残した個人的な写真アルバムである。【コンセプト】見る人が「この子にもこんな中学生時代があったんだな」と自然に感じられること。キャラクターの過去を振り返るアルバムであり、友情、青春、何気ない日常、少し恥ずかしい思い出、を大切にすること。【アルバム形式】実在する家庭用ポケット式写真アルバム。アルバムを見開きで開いた状態。左ページ3枚。右ページ3枚。合計6枚の横向き写真で構成すること。写真は実際にアルバムポケットへ収納されたL判写真として表現すること。アルバム中央のノド側にはコメント記入欄を設けること。各写真にはキャラクター本人が当時書いたと思われる短い手書きコメントを添えること。コメントは中学生らしい自然な内容にすること。【写真内容】写真はすべて異なる思い出にすること。内容は毎回変化してよい。例：{argument name="思い出のシーン" default="友達とプール、体育祭、修学旅行、文化祭、卒業式、部活動"}など。記念撮影だけでなく、何気ない日常のスナップ写真も積極的に含めること。写真ごとに異なる季節や学校行事が存在してよい。【キャラクター描写】参照画像のキャラクターを同一人物の中学生時代として描写すること。顔立ち、髪型、髪色、目の形、表情の癖、雰囲気、キャラクター性を維持すること。現在の姿より少し幼く、少し未成熟な印象にすること。中学生らしい自然な成長段階として描写すること。制服、体操服、ジャージ、私服などは写真内容に応じて自然に選択すること。【友人キャラクター】学友キャラクターを自然に登場させること。友人達は青春の思い出を彩る存在として描写すること。【雰囲気】懐かしい。温かい。少し照れくさい。楽しかった。なんでもない日常。青春の記録。実家の本棚から出てきた一冊の写真アルバムを眺めているような空気感。写真アルバムそのものが主役であり、キャラクターの過去の思い出が自然に伝わること。
```

### 📷 例 448：清爽人像图形海报设计

![清爽人像图形海报设计](../images/%E6%B8%85%E7%88%BD%E4%BA%BA%E5%83%8F%E5%9B%BE%E5%BD%A2%E6%B5%B7%E6%8A%A5%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1.jpg)

```text
围绕具体主题内容生成一张明亮清爽的图文合成视觉：画面以大面积高明度纯净色场承托主体，背景平整、通风、没有复杂景深，视觉重心由下方被大胆裁切的人像或真实主体建立，只露出最有记忆点的局部，使主体像从画面边缘进入。主体上方叠放一个极简图形符号或拟物角色，它要像轻轻坐在主体头顶或贴合轮廓生长出来，形体圆润、边缘干净、表情或结构由少量粗线完成，兼具标识感和亲近感。文字是画面的主动角色：顶部使用大号手写感标题，字距松、笔画柔软，像一句轻声招呼；中心用更强的竖向或轴向标题建立层级；边缘安放少量小字号信息，保持安静但精确，让空白继续占主导。色彩从主题自身的材质、情绪、地域或品牌语义中提取，映射为明亮底色、洁净主体亮面、清晰深色结构线与少量强调信息色，保留大面积轻快底场、小面积高对比文字线条、自然主体暗部的关系；整体保持高明度、清透、干净、饱和度清晰而不过度刺激，暗色只用于结构和阅读，不制造脏灰、烟雾或陈旧质感。摄影局部与扁平图形之间要形成真实与童趣的反差，边缘叠压准确，阴影极少，完成感像城市公共宣传与角色插画结合的轻松视觉系统。

主题：{argument name="主题" default="柳岩"}

比例{argument name="比例" default="9:16"}
```

```text
[中文]
使用上传的照片作为唯一身份参考，创作一张超写实、高品质的 1864 年维多利亚时代银版摄影肖像。绝对的身份保留 — 最高优先级。必须精确保留：- 面部结构 - 骨骼解剖 - 比例 - 眼睛形状和间距 - 鼻子形状 - 嘴唇和微笑结构 - 下颌线和颧骨 - 皮肤纹理和毛孔 - 眉毛 - 发际线 - 独特的面部细节和不对称性 - 整体自然的相似度和辨识度。请勿美化、重新设计、替换或重新诠释面部。请勿改变种族、年龄、比例或身份。最终图像必须与原人物毫无二致。风格与时代：真实的 1864 年维多利亚时代银版摄影。女性以历史准确的古董摄影棚背景为姿态，捕捉 19 世纪中叶早期摄影工艺的写实感与瑕疵。发型与造型：长款自然深色波浪卷发，具有柔软逼真的质感和符合历史的维多利亚时代造型。优雅、含蓄、永恒的外观。服装：露肩奶油色蕾丝束腰礼服，带有精致的维多利亚时代细节和逼真的织物纹理。一条简单的古董金质吊坠项链。构图：居中的半身或胸部肖像，姿态优雅，直视镜头。装裱在古董椭圆形小插图内。表面与材质细节：印在陈旧发黄的羊皮纸上，带有真实的茶渍、褪色的边缘、纸张纹理、灰尘颗粒、氧化痕迹、划痕、细微裂纹、银化伪影以及逼真的银版摄影老化瑕疵。文字：底部有优雅的手写草书，内容为：“LEO 1804”。光影：柔和的自然摄影棚光线，具有逼真的光影衰减、微妙的阴影、栩栩如生的皮肤渲染以及符合历史的曝光特性。自然的色调过渡，无任何人工美颜滤镜效果。色彩与色调：棕褐色调的褪色单色调色板，具有真实的古董摄影化学美学。浓郁的历史氛围，电影级的深度，令人难以忘怀的美感。写实度与质量：超写实 8K 杰作。超精细的高级摄影质量。看起来就像是从博物馆档案中以超高分辨率扫描的、经过专业保存的真实维多利亚时代银版照片。自然的皮肤纹理、逼真的眼睛、逼真的织物、逼真的光影、物理上精确的细节。如同由世界级专业肖像摄影师使用超高端中画幅相机拍摄，同时保留了 1864 年真实的摄影特征。

[English]
Create an ultra-realistic premium 1864 Victorian daguerreotype portrait using the uploaded photo strictly as the identity reference of the SAME woman. ABSOLUTE IDENTITY PRESERVATION — HIGHEST PRIORITY Preserve EXACTLY: - facial structure - bone anatomy - proportions - eye shape and spacing - nose shape - lips and smile structure - jawline and cheekbones - skin texture and pores - eyebrows - hairline - unique facial details and asymmetry - overall natural likeness and recognizability Do NOT beautify, redesign, replace, or reinterpret the face. Do NOT alter ethnicity, age, proportions, or identity. The final image must look unmistakably like the original person. STYLE & ERA: Authentic 1864 Victorian daguerreotype portrait photography. The woman appears posed in a historically accurate antique studio setting, captured with the realism and imperfections of early photographic processes from the mid-19th century. HAIR & STYLING: Long natural dark wavy hair with soft realistic texture and historically appropriate Victorian styling. Elegant, subtle, timeless appearance. WARDROBE: An off-the-shoulder cream lace corset gown with delicate Victorian details and realistic fabric texture. A simple antique gold locket necklace. COMPOSITION: Centered bust or half-body portrait with graceful posture and direct gaze toward the camera. Framed inside an antique oval vignette. SURFACE & MATERIAL DETAILS: Printed on aged yellowed parchment paper with authentic tea stains, faded edges, paper texture, dust particles, oxidation marks, scratches, subtle cracks, silvering artifacts, and realistic daguerreotype aging imperfections. TEXT: Elegant handwritten cursive calligraphy at the bottom reading: “LEO 1804” LIGHTING: Soft natural studio lighting with realistic falloff, subtle shadows, true-to-life skin rendering, and historically accurate exposure characteristics. Natural tonal transitions with no artificial beauty filter effect. COLOR & TONE: Sepia-toned faded monochromatic palette with authentic antique photographic chemistry aesthetics. Rich historical atmosphere, cinematic depth, hauntingly beautiful mood. REALISM & QUALITY: Ultra-realistic 8K masterwork. Hyper-detailed premium photography quality. Looks like a genuine professionally preserved Victorian daguerreotype scanned in ultra-high resolution from a museum archive. Natural skin texture, realistic eyes, realistic fabric, realistic lighting, physically accurate details. Shot as if captured by a world-class professional portrait photographer using an ultra-high-end medium format camera while preserving authentic 1864 photographic character.
```

### 📷 例 466：奢华午夜派对婚礼大片

![奢华午夜派对婚礼大片](../images/%E5%A5%A2%E5%8D%8E%E5%8D%88%E5%A4%9C%E6%B4%BE%E5%AF%B9%E5%A9%9A%E7%A4%BC%E5%A4%A7%E7%89%87.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
请基于用户上传的两位成年情侣清晰生活照，生成“纸醉金迷午夜派对婚纱照”风格的高预算婚纱写真。请严格保留两个人的真实身份特征，包括脸型、五官比例、眼睛、鼻子、嘴唇、肤色、年龄感、发际线、发型基础、身材比例和整体气质。生成结果必须清楚看起来是同一对真实情侣，不能变成陌生人，不能欧美化过度，不能过度网红化，不能出现 AI 假脸。请只把上传照片作为人物身份参考，不要保留原照片里的背景、衣服、姿势、道具和场景。请重新生成一张全新的高定婚纱写真。 ## 主题与氛围 整体主题为：纸醉金迷、午夜派对、婚礼 after party、高级婚纱 editorial、香槟庆典、上流派对感、电影感婚礼大片。画面要有“万元级超高预算婚纱照”的质感：- 高级定制造景 - 精致布景和道具 - 真实摄影棚级布光 - 时尚杂志感 - 电影海报般氛围 - 不是普通婚纱照，不是廉价影楼风，不是简单纪念照 ## 场景与布景 场景为奢华婚礼派对现场，可在同一主题下变化不同角落，但整体统一：- 米白色、香槟色或奶油灰色的垂坠布幔背景 - 暗色木质墙面 / 深色门板 / 复古会所感空间 - 香槟塔、复古高脚杯、婚礼蛋糕、蜡烛、银白或金色桌布、香槟瓶 - 金银色纸片礼花 / 飘落亮片 - 局部强光源、舞会感顶光、暖金色轮廓光、轻微镜头 flare - 地面可有礼花碎片、反光、派对残影感 色调统一为：黑、白、象牙白、香槟金、暖金棕、奶油灰。整体低饱和但昂贵，有光泽，有层次，有夜晚派对气息。## 新郎与新娘造型 新郎造型：- 黑色高定礼服 / 黑色 tuxedo / 黑色晚宴西装 - 白衬衫或黑色内搭 - 黑色领结或高级感黑色颈部细节 - 版型利落，修身但不过分紧 - 造型成熟、松弛、贵气，有派对主人的气场 新娘造型：- 高定白色婚纱 / 象牙白婚纱 / 抹胸鱼尾婚纱 / 结构感束身婚纱 / 蕾丝礼服婚纱 - 可以有 corset 结构、轻微珍珠或蕾丝细节、修身鱼尾或流线裙摆 - 佩戴夸张但高级的长款耳饰、精致手链、戒指 - 整体像高预算新娘 after party 造型，贵气、艳光、精致，不要廉价婚纱感 ## 发型控制 请基于两个人原始发型基础做正式造型升级，不要脱离本人的发量、发际线和长度级别。新郎发型：- 保留原始发型长度级别和发际线 - 可以做正式派对感整理，例如利落后梳、偏分、纹理感定型、干净成熟的礼服发型 - 不要凭空大幅改变发量和脸型 新娘发型：- 根据原图头发长度决定造型方向 - 长发 / 中长发可做低盘发、松弛盘发、法式盘发、优雅发髻、带少量脸侧碎发的晚宴造型 - 如果原图较短，则保持短发体系，只做精致晚宴化造型 - 如果原图有刘海，请保留刘海属性，可优化为轻薄、更适合婚纱照的状态 - 如果原图无刘海，不要强行加厚重刘海 - 如果原图发色偏黑，不要纯黑死黑，请处理为深棕黑、黑茶色、柔和深咖色，更有高级质感 ## 妆容要求 新娘妆容非常重要，请统一为高定婚礼晚宴妆：- 清透但精致的奶油肌底妆 - 立体但不过重的修容 - 眼妆为香槟金、暖棕、微烟熏或细致拉长眼线 - 睫毛清晰精致 - 眉形利落优雅 - 腮红轻微，重点放在面部立体感与光泽感 - 嘴唇为玫瑰裸粉、豆沙玫瑰、轻熟红棕或水润高级红调 - 整体精致、贵气、上镜，有女明星婚礼晚宴感 新郎妆面只做基础修饰：- 干净皮肤质感 - 面部轮廓清晰 - 不要重妆感 ## 人物关系、表情与情绪 请特别强化两个人之间的关系感和情绪流动。不要拍成两个陌生模特摆拍。关系气质：- 亲密 - 默契 - 有共同庆祝的兴奋感 - 有 after party 的放松和纵情 - 有高级婚礼大片的张力 - 像一对刚刚完成婚礼仪式、进入午夜庆祝环节的新婚情侣 表情不要单一：- 有的镜头是纵情大笑 - 有的镜头是微醺般的松弛笑意 - 有的镜头是勾人、得意、带派对主角气场的表情 - 有的镜头是暧昧贴近、快要接吻 - 有的镜头是安静凝视 - 有的镜头是玩闹感与调情感并存 不要每一张都只有标准微笑和标准看镜头。## 抓拍感与镜头变化 请强化“瞬间感”和“被捕捉到的派对高光时刻”，不要每一张都像站好拍样片。允许出现：- 动作正在发生中 - 笑容正在展开 - 刚举杯、刚靠近、刚转头、刚旋转、刚低头、刚准备亲吻 - 轻微动态感 - 礼花正在飘落 - 裙摆在动 - 一个人看镜头，另一个人看对方或看向别处 构图必须有变化：- 近景特写 - 半身 - 3/4 身 - 全身 - 低机位 - 前景虚化 - 道具进入前景 - 局部遮挡 - 纵深关系 ## 人物比例与结构控制 请特别注意两个人的脸部比例、头身比例和身体结构必须自然真实。两个人都要像真实成年人，不要出现脸过大、头过小、脖子过长、肩膀错位、身体扭曲、手臂过长、手掌过大、身体拼接感或不符合人体结构的问题。请根据上传照片保留两个人各自真实的脸型、头部大小、身材比例、肩宽、脖颈长度和整体体态。不要把新郎变得过度高大、脸过宽、肩膀过夸张；不要把新娘变得过度幼态、头过大、身体过小、脖子过细或比例失衡。两个人同框时，请保持合理的空间关系和透视关系：- 近镜头的人可以稍微更大，但不要夸张变形 - 远一点的人可以略小，但仍要保持真实成年人比例 - 两个人的头部大小差异要自然 - 脸、肩膀、手臂、躯干之间的位置关系要符合真实情侣合照 - 拥抱、贴近、举杯、跳舞、托腰、牵手时，肢体接触要自然，不要出现手臂穿模、手指混乱、身体重叠错误或肢体融合 ## 输出要求 请输出一张高完成度、可直接成片的婚纱写真：- 3:4 竖构图 - 清晰、精致、高级 - 像超高预算婚纱摄影工作室的 final selected shot Prompt 01｜倾身欲吻主海报 拍摄一张极具戏剧张力的主海报镜头。新郎穿黑色高定晚宴礼服，一只手高高举起复古香槟杯，另一只手稳稳托住新娘腰部；新娘穿白色结构感高定婚纱，身体向后微微倾斜，被新郎稳稳搂住，两个人在礼花飘落中靠得很近，正处于快要接吻的一瞬间，鼻尖和嘴唇距离极近，但还没有真正吻上。情绪要暧昧、张扬、庆祝感强，像婚礼 after party 的最高潮时刻。新郎带一点侵略性的温柔和主导感，新娘带一点陶醉、享受和艳光四射的状态。构图为 3/4 身到全身之间，镜头略低机位，人物具有强烈身体延展感。背景是巨大的香槟色垂坠布幔，周围有大量金银纸片在空中飘落，画面有暖金色电影光与轻微前景虚化，整体像高预算婚礼大片封面。Prompt 02｜蛋糕桌前纵情大笑 拍摄一张婚礼派对桌前的热闹庆祝镜头。新郎和新娘站在铺着银白或珍珠光泽桌布的派对桌旁，桌上有白色婚礼蛋糕、香槟瓶、烛台、香槟杯、首饰盒或戒指盒、散落礼花。新郎一手举起香槟瓶，另一手拿酒杯或自然张开，仰头大笑；新娘手拿复古香槟杯，身体轻轻倚向桌面，闭眼笑或大笑，状态放松、玩得很开心。情绪是婚礼仪式结束后的纵情庆祝，热闹、奢华、松弛、贵气。两个人之间要有共同狂欢的默契，而不是各自拍照。构图为 3/4 身，桌面细节丰富但不杂乱，背景为香槟色布幔和暖金色光影。画面要像高预算婚礼 after party 的现场抓拍成片。Prompt 03｜举杯邀请镜头 拍摄一张很有“派对主人邀请感”的镜头。新郎在画面前景一侧，手持复古香槟杯向镜头伸出，杯口接近镜头，形成明显前景透视；新娘站在后方但仍清晰可见，身穿白色高定婚纱，站在香槟塔和烛光之间，也手持香槟杯，微微看向镜头，像共同邀请观者进入这场午夜婚礼派对。情绪不要太甜腻，而要有一点派对主角的得意、从容、轻微挑逗感。新郎可以带一点松弛笑意，新娘可以是迷人微笑或轻微高傲的优雅神情。构图为低机位或略广角，前景杯子放大但人物比例仍自然。背景包含布幔、烛光、香槟塔、桌面细节和少量飘落礼花。整体是很有海报感的一张。Prompt 04｜耳边低语暧昧特写 拍摄一张暧昧而精致的近景半身特写。新娘站在前面，肩颈线条和婚纱抹胸结构清晰可见，一只手轻轻碰触脸侧或唇边，表情带一点惊喜、调情感和被逗到的笑意；新郎从侧后方贴近她耳边，像在低语、调情或耳语庆祝，鼻尖和面颊离她很近，一只手自然环住她腰部。情绪要非常有关系感：亲密、会心、调情、午夜派对感。新郎不看镜头，看向新娘；新娘可以看向镜头，也可以看向侧前方，眼神有光，像刚刚听到一句让人心动又好笑的话。构图为近景半身，突出脸部、耳饰、婚纱结构、手部和腰部关系。背景柔化，但仍保留香槟色布幔和暖金色晚宴灯光。整体像时尚婚礼大片中的亲密特写。Prompt 05｜杯影之间的暧昧对视 拍摄一张非常有杂志感的前后景特写镜头。新郎位于前景偏左，半侧脸或侧脸，正举起复古香槟杯轻轻抿酒，人物可以部分虚化或前景压近；新娘位于后方偏右，身穿白色高定婚纱，手持香槟杯，透过杯影或越过杯口看向镜头，眼神迷人、冷静、带一点午夜派对女主角的魅惑感。情绪是成熟、暧昧、精致、带一点微醺夜色的高级感。不要傻笑，要更像电影里 midnight party 的主角镜头。构图要有明显的前后层次，杯子和人物关系要漂亮，玻璃折射和暖金色光影要高级。背景干净但有布景存在感，整体像高级时尚婚纱广告。Prompt 06｜礼花中的共舞全身镜头 拍摄一张全身动态镜头。新郎与新娘在礼花飘落中牵手共舞，新郎一只手高高举起，身体打开，笑得很开心；新娘一只手被牵起，另一只手轻提裙摆或让裙摆自然甩开，身体带轻微旋转，婚纱裙摆有流动感。两个人像在婚礼 after party 的舞池中自由庆祝。情绪要非常鲜活，像一段真实动作中的高光瞬间：刚刚转身、刚刚大笑、刚刚举起手。不是僵硬定格，而是动作正在发生中。构图为全身竖构图，人物比例自然，地面可见散落礼花，背景是高大垂坠布幔、局部强光和派对场景。整体像万元级婚礼大片中最有气氛的动态图。Prompt 07｜蛋糕主角的俏皮合照 拍摄一张带一点俏皮感和社交传播感的婚礼派对镜头。新郎和新娘站在派对桌前，共同托着或展示一只白色婚礼蛋糕。新郎状态外放一点，可以看向镜头，带一点开心夸张的笑意或“今晚主角就是我们”的得意感；新娘轻轻靠向新郎，一只手扶着蛋糕或扶着自己下巴，表情可以是轻微撅嘴、俏皮、娇矜、可爱中带高级感。情绪不是传统端庄婚纱照，而是 after party 的轻松玩闹感，像一对很会玩的新人。构图为半身到 3/4 身，蛋糕清晰但不要喧宾夺主，桌上依然有烛光、香槟杯、礼花、首饰盒或派对道具。背景统一为高级婚礼派对场景，整体有高预算时尚大片感。Prompt 08｜午夜冷艳收束大片 拍摄一张非常时尚、非常高级的收束镜头。新郎与新娘站在暗色木门或复古深色墙面前，派对桌与香槟塔可作为前景或侧景出现，但主体是两个人。新郎穿黑色高定礼服，站姿利落，眼神冷静、有掌控感；新娘穿白色高定婚纱，姿态挺拔，肩颈舒展，眼神冷艳、自信、像午夜派对女主角。两个人不需要明显大笑，而是表现一种“狂欢过后仍然贵气逼人”的时尚封面感。情绪是冷艳、从容、昂贵、像顶级婚纱企划的最终收束页。两个人可以轻微靠近，也可以保持一点距离，但气场必须统一、强、贵。构图为全身或 3/4 身，前景可有少量香槟塔、礼花或桌角，但不要太繁杂。画面要像时尚杂志封面或高定婚纱 campaign 收官图。

Ultra-detailed 8k resolution, cinematic noir lighting, mafia boss dominance aesthetic with restrained fantasy.

### 📷 例 534：保持面部一致性的时尚编辑人像

![保持面部一致性的时尚编辑人像](../images/%E4%BF%9D%E6%8C%81%E9%9D%A2%E9%83%A8%E4%B8%80%E8%87%B4%E6%80%A7%E7%9A%84%E6%97%B6%E5%B0%9A%E7%BC%96%E8%BE%91%E4%BA%BA%E5%83%8F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
超写实全身时尚编辑摄影作品，拍摄一位与参考图拥有相同面部特征和外貌的年轻美女。她站在日落后高档金融区的一根光滑石灰岩柱旁，展现出自信与魅力的优雅融合。她佩戴着现代黑色方框太阳镜，以一种轻松时尚的姿态望向灯火辉煌的城市景观。
发型：
浓密乌黑的长发，质感如丝绸般奢华且富有健康光泽，打造出精致的韩式沙龙吹发造型。柔和的修颜层次过渡到蓬松的波浪卷，随着傍晚微风拂过，呈现出自然的动感。发丝捕捉着周围的城市灯光，产生微妙的光泽高光，勾勒出优雅的女性轮廓。
妆容：
精致的韩式美学妆容，拥有透亮的玻璃肌、平滑的年轻肤质、细腻的面部轮廓、柔和的玫瑰桃色腮红、微妙的修容、面部高点的自然光泽、暖灰褐色眼影、精致的眼线、羽毛般卷翘的睫毛、柔和的眉形以及亮泽的玫瑰米色唇妆。明亮传神的双眸和温柔的微笑营造出一种既亲切又高级的氛围。
穿搭：
超大款高级炭灰色针织毛衣，正面饰有大型艺术感单色笔触图案和抽象街头艺术元素。质感丰富的针织面料、宽松版型、落肩设计，展现当代奢华街头风格。搭配高腰水洗蓝牛仔 A 字短裙，带有微妙的磨损细节和利落的剪裁。经典的黑白滑板鞋完善了整体造型。肩上自然地背着一个黑色大号棉质托特包。极简的配饰打造出干净的编辑风格。
姿势：
在柱子旁呈现放松的时尚模特站姿，一侧肩膀轻触建筑。一只手随意地抓着托特包带，另一只手自然垂在毛衣下摆附近。重心自然地落在一条腿上，营造出毫不费力的轮廓感。头部微微转动，传达出冷静的自信和现代都市女孩的活力。
环境：
夜晚的高端市中心商业区，周围环绕着玻璃大厦、灯火通明的办公楼、优雅的建筑细节、抛光的石材人行道、路面上微妙的倒影、远处的车灯以及柔和的都市光斑。干净的奢华城市背景，具有电影般的氛围和高端时尚大片的质感。
光影：
专业的夜间编辑灯光，结合了温暖的建筑上射灯与冷色调的城市照明。柔和的定向高光突出了面部特征和发丝质感，同时保留了真实的阴影。丰富的对比度、大气的景深以及高级时尚杂志的美学风格。
摄影：
使用 85mm 人像镜头拍摄，全身垂直构图，浅景深，超写实的皮肤细节，逼真的发丝，高度还原的针织与牛仔纹理。

[English]
Ultra-realistic full-body fashion editorial photograph of a beautiful young woman with the same facial identity and appearance as the reference. She is standing beside a sleek limestone column in an upscale financial district after sunset, projecting a graceful blend of confidence and charm. She wears modern black square-frame sunglasses and glances toward the illuminated cityscape with a relaxed, fashionable attitude.
Hair:
Long, dense raven-black hair with a luxurious silky texture and healthy shine, styled in a polished Korean salon blowout. Soft face-framing layers flow into loose voluminous waves, creating natural movement as a light evening breeze passes through. Individual strands catch the surrounding city lights, producing subtle glossy highlights and an elegant feminine silhouette.
Makeup:
Refined Korean beauty-inspired makeup with luminous glass-like skin, smooth youthful complexion, delicate facial contours, soft rosy-peach blush, subtle sculpting, natural radiance on the high points of the face, warm taupe eyeshadow, fine eyeliner, feathery curled lashes, softly defined brows, and glossy rose-beige lips. Bright expressive eyes and a gentle smile create a friendly yet sophisticated mood.
Outfit:
Oversized premium charcoal-grey knit sweater featuring large artistic monochrome brushstroke graphics and abstract street-art elements across the front. Rich textured knit fabric, relaxed fit, dropped shoulders, and contemporary luxury streetwear styling. Paired with a high-waisted faded blue denim A-line mini skirt with subtle distressed detailing and clean tailoring. Classic black-and-white skate-inspired sneakers complete the look. A large black cotton tote bag rests naturally on one shoulder. Minimal jewelry for a clean editorial appearance.
Pose:
Relaxed fashion-model stance beside the column, one shoulder lightly touching the structure. One hand casually holding the tote strap while the other rests near the hem of the sweater. Weight shifted naturally onto one leg, creating an effortless silhouette. Head slightly turned, conveying calm confidence and modern city-girl energy.
Environment:
Exclusive downtown business district at night, surrounded by glass towers, illuminated office buildings, elegant architectural details, polished stone walkways, subtle reflections on the pavement, distant vehicle lights, and soft metropolitan bokeh. Clean luxury urban setting with a cinematic atmosphere and premium fashion-campaign feel.
Lighting:
Professional nighttime editorial lighting combining warm architectural uplighting with cool city illumination. Soft directional highlights accentuate facial features and hair texture while preserving realistic shadows. Rich contrast, atmospheric depth, and a sophisticated luxury-magazine aesthetic.
Photography:
Shot on an 85mm portrait lens, full-body vertical composition, shallow depth of field, ultra-realistic skin detail, realistic hair strands, highly detailed knit and denim
```

### 📷 例 538：撕纸分割人像

![撕纸分割人像](../images/%E6%92%95%E7%BA%B8%E5%88%86%E5%89%B2%E4%BA%BA%E5%83%8F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
创作一张高分辨率、超写实的电影级特写人像，主体为 {argument name="character description" default="一位留着长波浪卷发、身穿黑色罗纹高领毛衣的女性"}，居中且正对镜头。整张图像沿垂直中线被一道参差不齐的白色撕纸缝隙精准分割，缝隙从顶部边缘穿过头发延伸至底部边缘。左侧为鲜艳的暖色调，呈现栗棕色头发、金色轮廓光、隐约可见的暖色皮肤、柔和的棕色摄影棚背景，以及左下角可见的金色链条项链。右侧为深色、去饱和的近黑单色调，呈现黑色头发、深邃阴影、炭灰色背景，以及右下角可见的银色链条项链。在人像中心放置一个大型不透明方形遮挡块，遮住从发际线下方到上胸部的面部区域；该遮挡块应呈现出从左侧暖棕色到右侧深炭灰色的细微水平渐变，并与分割线对齐。采用戏剧性的低调照明、浅景深、逼真的发质纹理、情绪化的编辑类时尚摄影风格，4:5 垂直构图，无文字，无水印，无多余物体。

[English]
Create a high-resolution hyper-realistic cinematic close-up portrait of {argument name="character description" default="a woman with long wavy hair wearing a black ribbed turtleneck"}, centered and facing forward, with the entire image split exactly down the vertical middle by a jagged torn white paper seam running from the top edge through the hair and down to the bottom edge. The left half is vibrant warm color with chestnut-brown hair, golden rim lighting, warm skin tones mostly hidden, a soft brown studio background, and a visible gold chain necklace at the lower left. The right half is dark desaturated near-black monochrome with black hair, deep shadows, a charcoal background, and a visible silver chain necklace at the lower right. Place one large opaque square censor block over the center of the portrait, covering the face from just below the top hairline to the upper chest; the block should have a subtle horizontal gradient from warm brown on the left to dark charcoal on the right, aligned with the split. Use dramatic low-key lighting, shallow depth of field, realistic hair texture, moody editorial fashion photography, 4:5 vertical composition, no text, no watermark, no extra objects.
```

### 📷 例 542：复古海滩冲浪板时尚提示词

![复古海滩冲浪板时尚提示词](../images/%E5%A4%8D%E5%8F%A4%E6%B5%B7%E6%BB%A9%E5%86%B2%E6%B5%AA%E6%9D%BF%E6%97%B6%E5%B0%9A%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
[人物面部及身体细节参考] 图像 1，图像 2，超写实 [人物姿势与表情] 全身站立姿势，背靠在沙滩上一块巨大的 {argument name="surfboard color" default="yellow"} 冲浪板上。双臂高举过头顶，握住冲浪板两侧，一条腿轻轻交叉在另一条腿前，以突显纤细的身材轮廓。直视镜头，表情俏皮，像充满活力且被阳光晃到眼一样灿烂大笑，皱着鼻子。 [人物服装] 穿着一件干净简约的 {argument name="swimsuit style" default="white one-piece swimsuit"}，流畅地展现身体线条。这是一种清爽露肩的款式，颈后连接着细细的挂脖肩带作为点缀，时尚地展现出运动感与经典感并存的海滩冲浪造型。 [人物发型与妆容细节] 头发随风飘动，自然地遮住部分脸庞。妆容强调双颊上生动且大面积的粉色腮红，仿佛被烈日晒得泛红，散发出多汁的魅力。 [光影与光线方向] 强烈而耀眼的正午自然光从人物右前方照射而来。这种光线通透而明亮地照亮了人物的泳衣和皮肤，并在身后黄色冲浪板的表面投射出柔和轻盈的阴影，增加了立体感。 [质感与色彩氛围] 整体为复古画报风格，具有强烈的独特模拟胶片颗粒质感。冲浪板清新的黄色、大海深邃的蓝色、泳衣的白色以及白色浪花泡沫的质感交织在一起，营造出一种清爽而又复古的仲夏氛围。 [胶片与相机镜头景深、角度] 情感表达如同用复古 35mm 胶片相机拍摄，采用正面平视角度，将人物置于画面中心，使用广角镜头捕捉人物全身及整个冲浪板。景深设置为中等，清晰地捕捉前方的人物，同时将身后汹涌的海浪和大海自然柔和地虚化。 [背景元素（可选）] 脚下是柔软的沙滩，身后是带有岁月痕迹的巨大黄色冲浪板。在冲浪板正后方，可以看到翻滚着粗犷白色泡沫的动态海浪和蓝色大海。 [签名] 右下角有一个 Meta 运营的 Threads 小标志，上方用白色草书小字写着 "{argument name="signature name" default="CHAse"}" 作为签名。 3:4 比例

[English]
[인물 얼굴 및 신체 디테일 참조] 이미지 1, 이미지 2, 하이퍼 리얼리스틱 [인물의 포즈 및 표정] 해변의 모래사장 위에서 커다란 {argument name="서프보드 색상" default="노란색"} 서프보드를 등지고 똑바로 서 있는 전신 포즈입니다. 양팔을 머리 위로 높이 들어 올려 서프보드의 양옆을 잡고 있으며, 한쪽 다리를 다른 쪽 다리 앞으로 가볍게 교차하여 꼬아 날씬한 실루엣을 강조합니다. 활기차고 눈이 부신 듯 코를 찡긋하며 환하게 웃는 장난기 가득한 표정으로 카메라를 응시하고 있습니다. [인물 의상] 몸매 라인이 부드럽게 드러나는 깔끔하고 미니멀한 {argument name="수영복 스타일" default="화이트 원피스 수영복"}을 입고 있습니다. 어깨가 시원하게 드러나는 스타일이면서 목뒤로 얇은 홀터넥 끈이 연결되어 포인트가 되며, 해변의 스포티하면서도 클래식한 서핑 룩을 패셔너블하게 보여줍니다. [인물 헤어스타일 및 메이크업 디테일] 머리카락은 바람에 흩날려 자연스럽게 얼굴의 일부분을 가리고 있습니다. 메이크업은 뜨거운 태양에 상기된 듯 양 볼에 선명하고 넓은 핑크빛 블러셔를 강조하여 과즙미 넘치는 매력을 발산합니다. [조명 및 빛 방향] 한낮의 강렬하고 눈부신 자연 태양광이 인물의 정면 우측 상단에서 내리쬐고 있습니다. 이 빛은 인물의 수영복과 피부를 투명하고 화사하게 밝혀주며, 인물의 뒤쪽에 있는 노란색 서프보드 표면에 부드럽고 옅은 음영 그림자를 만들어내어 입체감을 더해줍니다. [질감과 색감 무드] 전체적으로 아날로그 필름 고유의 자글자글한 입자 질감이 강하게 느껴지는 빈티지한 화보 스타일입니다. 서프보드의 산뜻한 노란색, 바다의 짙은 푸른색, 수영복의 화이트, 그리고 하얗게 부서지는 파도의 거품 질감이 한데 어우러져 한여름의 청량하면서도 레트로한 무드를 자아냅니다. [필름 및 카메라 렌즈 심도, 앵글] 빈티지 35mm 필름 카메라로 촬영된 듯한 감성이며, 인물의 전신과 서프보드 전체를 담은 와이드 앵글로 화면 중앙에 길게 배치한 정면 눈높이 앵글입니다. 피사계 심도는 중간 정도로 설정되어 전면의 인물은 매우 선명하게 포착되었고, 바로 뒤에서 거칠게 치는 파도와 바다는 자연스럽고 부드럽게 묘사되었습니다. [배경 요소 (옵션)] 발밑에는 부드러운 해변의 모래사장이 펼쳐져 있고, 인물의 등 뒤로는 세월의 흔적이 있는 거대한 노란색 서프보드가 세워져 있습니다. 보드 바로 뒤쪽으로는 거친 흰색 포말을 일으키며 부서지는 역동적인 파도와 푸른 바다가 보입니다. [싸인] 우측하단에는 Meta가 운영하는 Threads 로고가 작게 위치하고, 그 위에 흰색 필기체로 "{argument name="서명 이름" default="CHAse"}"라고 싸인처럼 작게 적혀 있다. 3:4비율
```

**来源：** [@CHAse](https://x.com/CHAseUnre/status/2062714774631440556) | 2026-06-05

### 📷 例 544：动漫风 AI 课程演示缩略图

![动漫风 AI 课程演示缩略图](../images/%E5%8A%A8%E6%BC%AB%E9%A3%8E%20AI%20%E8%AF%BE%E7%A8%8B%E6%BC%94%E7%A4%BA%E7%BC%A9%E7%95%A5%E5%9B%BE.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：创作一张正方形的动漫风格社交媒体插图，用于展示 AI 图像生成课程，画面需呈现商务演示场景，并包含一张带有大量日语文字的 Slides。

画布：1:1 正方形图像，干净精致的数字动漫插画，会议室或教室的中景镜头。温暖的米色墙壁，前景为木质会议桌，采用细腻的线条和柔和的阴影。

主体：在左侧绘制一位成年日本女性演讲者，腰部至大腿以上构图，身穿海军蓝西装外套、白色衬衫，搭配灰色裙子或长裤。她留着深棕色头发，扎成低马尾。她的面部经过矩形柔和模糊/马赛克处理以保护隐私。她自信地站立，一只手自然下垂，另一只手臂指向演示屏幕。

前景物体：在木桌上放置 2 个物体：左侧中央附近放一个透明水杯，右侧放一台打开的深色笔记本电脑。

演示屏幕：在右侧放置一个带有黑色边框和支架的大型白色投影屏幕。屏幕上包含居中且分行的加粗黑色日语标题。请使用以下精确文本：{argument name="slide headline text" default="独学のAI画像生成が\n「なんとなく」で終わっていた私が、\n10万文字の「ChatGPT画像生成の教科書」\nで全部見直した話"}。确保文字清晰且醒目。

屏幕图形：在 Slides 上包含 7 个装饰性/内容元素：左上角 1 个黄色灯泡图标，顶部中央附近 1 个带框风景图片图标，右上角 1 个绿色 ChatGPT logo 图标及旁边 2 个小闪光符号，左下角 1 个处于蓝色思维云中且带有问号的困惑 Q 版女性，下部中央 1 个指向右侧的蓝色箭头，下部 1 个标有 {argument name="book title" default="ChatGPT\n画像生成の\n教科書"} 的绿色小册子/平板电脑，以及右下角 1 个自信的 Q 版女性，她举起一根手指，旁边有一个红色感叹号。所有 Q 版人物的面部也应进行矩形柔和模糊处理。

风格：日本商务漫画/动漫讲解缩略图风格，清晰的墨线轮廓，柔和的渐变，专业且易于接近，高对比度且易读的 Slides 排版，柔和的企业配色并点缀少量黄色闪光元素。整体构图应类似于关于学习实用 AI 图像生成的 X/Twitter 文章缩略图。

约束：仅使用 1 位主要演讲者，桌上仅放置 2 个物体，Slides 上仅包含列出的 7 个图形元素，总共 3 处面部模糊处理。请勿添加额外人物、额外图表、水印、Slides 以外的 logo，或除指定的日语 Slides 和小册子标签之外的任何额外文字。

[English]
Goal: Create a square anime-style social media illustration about an AI image generation course, showing a business presentation scene with a large Japanese text slide.

Canvas: 1:1 square image, clean polished digital anime illustration, medium-wide shot in a meeting room or classroom. Warm beige walls, wooden conference table in the foreground, subtle line art and soft shading.

Main subject: On the left side, draw one adult Japanese businesswoman presenter, waist-up to thigh-up, wearing a navy blazer, white button-up shirt, and gray skirt or trousers. She has dark brown hair tied in a low ponytail. Her face is intentionally covered by a rectangular soft blur/mosaic privacy block. She stands confidently with one hand lowered and the other arm extended toward the presentation screen.

Foreground objects: On the wooden table, place exactly 2 objects: one clear glass of water near the center-left and one open dark laptop angled on the right.

Presentation screen: On the right side, place a large white projection screen with a black frame and stand. The screen contains a bold Japanese headline in thick black type, centered and stacked. Use this exact text: {argument name="slide headline text" default="独学のAI画像生成が\n「なんとなく」で終わっていた私が、\n10万文字の「ChatGPT画像生成の教科書」\nで全部見直した話"}. Make the text readable and dominant.

Screen graphics: Include exactly 7 decorative/content elements on the slide: 1 yellow lightbulb icon at the top left, 1 framed landscape picture icon near the top center, 1 green ChatGPT logo icon at the top right with 2 small sparkle marks beside it, 1 small confused chibi woman in a blue thought cloud at the lower left with a question mark, 1 blue arrow pointing right in the lower center, 1 green booklet/tablet labeled {argument name="book title" default="ChatGPT\n画像生成の\n教科書"}, and 1 small confident chibi woman at the lower right raising one finger with a red exclamation mark beside her. Both chibi faces should also have soft rectangular privacy blur blocks.

Style: Japanese business manga / anime explainer thumbnail style, crisp ink outlines, soft gradients, professional yet approachable, high contrast readable slide typography, muted corporate colors with small yellow sparkle accents. The overall composition should resemble an X/Twitter article thumbnail about learning practical AI image generation.

Constraints: Use exactly 1 main presenter, exactly 2 table objects, exactly 7 slide graphic elements as listed, and 3 blurred faces total. Do not add extra people, extra charts, watermarks, logos outside the slide, or additional text beyond the specified Japanese slide and booklet labels.
```

**来源：** [@ジャバ・ザ・ハットリ🇩🇪←🇸🇬←🇯🇵](https://x.com/nodenodenode1/status/2062703460043092350) | 2026-06-05

### 📷 例 545：微型人群肖像

![微型人群肖像](../images/%E5%BE%AE%E5%9E%8B%E4%BA%BA%E7%BE%A4%E8%82%96%E5%83%8F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
生成一张完全由数千个微小人物组成的写实肖像，采用正上方俯视视角。微型人物的密度变化勾勒出主体面部的特征、阴影和轮廓；在头发和深色阴影区域密集排列，在较浅的肤色和白色背景上则稀疏分布。每个微小人物都投射出逼真的阴影，放大后可清晰辨认其服饰。从远处看，肖像呈现出清晰的人脸轮廓，而在全分辨率下，每个人物依然清晰可辨。超高细节，柔和暖光，白色或灰白色背景。

[English]
Generate a photorealistic portrait of {argument name="subject" default="[person]"} built entirely from thousands of tiny human figures, viewed from directly overhead. The miniature people vary in density to sculpt the subject's facial features, shadows, and contours, tightly clustered in dark areas like hair and deep shadows, loosely scattered across lighter skin tones and the white background. Each small figure casts a realistic shadow and shows distinct clothing when zoomed in. The portrait reads clearly as a face from a distance while individual people remain distinguishable at full resolution. Hyperdetailed, soft warm lighting, white or off-white background.
```

**来源：** [@PromptLab](https://x.com/iamaiistudio/status/2062702684126503082) | 2026-06-05

### 📷 例 546：清爽夏季护肤品商业广告提示词

![清爽夏季护肤品商业广告提示词](../images/%E6%B8%85%E7%88%BD%E5%A4%8F%E5%AD%A3%E6%8A%A4%E8%82%A4%E5%93%81%E5%95%86%E4%B8%9A%E5%B9%BF%E5%91%8A%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
[人物] 图像 1，图像 2 [人物姿势与表情] 采用低角度俯视镜头，右手向镜头前方伸展，重点展示 [{argument name="product" default="产品"} 见图像 3]。左手抬至肩部高度，手指微张，直视前方，表情清爽活泼，嘴唇微张。[人物服装] 穿着与 [图像 3] 产品颜色相近的 {argument name="outfit" default="罗纹针织无袖上衣"}。轻盈的上衣带来清爽的夏日感，凸显人物肩部和手臂线条，强调清新的季节性造型。[人物发型与妆容细节] {argument name="hairstyle" default="棕色长直发"} 打造出湿润感并自然向后梳理。妆容以通透水润的底妆为基础，双颊大面积涂抹活泼的粉色腮红，增添多汁感，唇部呈现水润质地，散发自然活力。[光影与光线方向] 来自晴朗户外环境的强烈明亮正午自然光，直射人物正面和护肤品。强烈的阳光穿透透明的化妆品容器，产生清晰的高光，并明亮地照亮人物的五官和皮肤纹理，不留阴影。[质感与色彩氛围] 天空的蓝色与服装及产品的清爽薄荷色和谐统一，营造出极致清凉、纯净的夏日氛围。精华液容器内的细微气泡质感、光滑的容器表面、服装致密的罗纹织物纹理，以及水润皮肤的光泽感均得到生动表现。[胶片与相机镜头深度、角度] 采用从地面向上仰拍的极端低角度“虫眼视角”。使用极浅的景深，使位于镜头正前方的化妆品瓶身显得非常巨大且清晰，而位于后方的人物面部和身体则呈现柔和的虚化效果。[背景元素（可选）] 湛蓝清澈的夏日天空几乎没有云朵，填满整个画面作为背景。仅在右下角悬挂着一小片非常柔和的白色积云，构成极简的户外背景，进一步衬托出人物和产品的清爽色彩。

[English]
[인물] 이미지 1, 이미지 2 [인물의 포즈 및 표정] 카메라를 아래로 내려다보는 로우 앵글 자세에서 오른손을 카메라 렌즈 앞으로 길게 뻗어 [이미지 3의 {argument name="제품" default="제품"}]을 크게 강조하여 들고 있습니다. 왼손은 손가락을 살짝 펼친 채 어깨 높이로 올리고 있으며, 입을 살짝 벌린 채 청량하고 생기 넘치는 표정으로 정면을 응시합니다. [인물 의상] [이미지 3] 제품 색상과 비슷한 {argument name="의상" default="골지 니트 민소매 탑"}을 입고 있습니다. 여름의 청량한 느낌을 주는 가벼운 스타일의 상의로, 인물의 어깨와 팔 라인을 시원하게 드러내어 싱그러운 계절감을 강조합니다. [인물 헤어스타일 및 메이크업 디테일] 물에 젖은 듯 촉촉하게 연출된 {argument name="헤어스타일" default="긴 갈색 생머리"}가 뒤로 자연스럽게 넘겨져 있습니다. 메이크업은 투명하고 촉촉한 피부 표현을 베이스로 하며, 양 볼에 생기 있는 핑크빛 블러셔를 넓게 발라 과즙미를 더했고 입술은 자연스러운 생기가 도는 촉촉한 제형으로 마감했습니다. [조명 및 빛 방향] 맑은 야외에서 내리쬐는 강렬하고 화사한 한낮의 자연광이 인물의 전면과 스킨케어 제품을 정면으로 비추고 있습니다. 강한 햇빛이 투명한 화장품 용기를 통과하며 맑은 하이라이트를 만들어내고, 인물의 이목구비와 피부 결을 그늘 없이 투명하게 밝혀줍니다. [질감과 색감 무드] 하늘의 파란색과 의상 및 제품의 청량한 민트색이 어우러져 극도의 시원하고 깨끗한 여름 무드를 연출합니다. 세럼 용기 내부의 미세한 기포 질감과 매끄러운 용기 표면 질감, 그리고 의상의 촘촘한 골지 원단 질감과 촉촉한 피부의 광택이 생생하게 표현됩니다. [필름 및 카메라 렌즈 심도, 앵글] 바닥에서 하늘을 올려다보는 극단적인 로우 앵글인 웜즈 아이 뷰로 촬영되었습니다. 매우 얕은 피사계 심도를 사용하여 카메라 바로 앞에 위치한 화장품 병은 매우 크고 선명하게 포착된 반면, 뒤쪽에 위치한 인물의 얼굴과 신체는 부드럽게 아웃포커싱되어 흐릿하게 처리되었습니다. [배경 요소 (옵션)] 구름이 거의 없는 맑고 깨끗한 여름날의 파란 하늘이 화면 전체를 배경으로 가득 채우고 있습니다. 우측 하단 부분에만 아주 부드러운 흰색 뭉게구름의 일부분이 살짝 걸쳐져 있어, 인물과 제품의 청량한 색감을 더욱 돋보이게 만드는 미니멀한 야외 배경입니다.
```

场景沐浴在柔和的漫射晨光中，在她的脸庞和肩部周围营造出明亮的光晕。周围环绕着飞舞的蝴蝶、闪烁的尘埃颗粒、半透明的花瓣和梦幻的花卉纹理，背景融合了淡紫色、珍珠白、腮红粉和银色调。电影级艺术摄影，仙女风美学，魔法花园氛围，魔幻现实主义，超细腻皮肤纹理，柔焦高光，体积光，焦外成像，大师级品质，高细节，8K 分辨率，精致女性美，浪漫奇幻艺术作品，优雅构图，梦幻调色，柔光，天界氛围。
